   The user asked to implement a 6-phase plan to simplify the work queue pipeline in `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/`. The goal is to make `build_work_queue()` readable top-to-bottom so debugging "why does item X appear?" requires reading one function in one file. The 6 phases are:
   - Phase 1: Kill `_resolve_plan_context` — always pass plan dict directly
   - Phase 2: Decompose `apply_plan_order` into named steps
   - Phase 3: Unify the sort into one pass (extend `item_sort_key` with plan position)
   - Phase 4: Move cluster collapse to callers
   - Phase 5: Split `helpers.py` junk drawer into `helpers.py` (predicates) + `synthetic.py` (builders/scoring)
   - Phase 6: Add pipeline docstring + wontfix regression test

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Work queue pipeline: filter findings by status, sort, return top N
   - Plan-aware ordering: `queue_order` list determines planned item positions
   - Unified sort key: `_plan_position` and `_is_new` stamped on items, consumed by `item_sort_key`
   - Cluster collapse: display concern moved from builder to callers

3. Files and Code Sections:

     ```python
     new_ids: set[str] = set()
     skipped_items: list[dict] = []
     if resolved_options.plan:
         new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
         _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
         _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, resolved_options.plan, new_ids)
         all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
             all_items, resolved_options.plan,

             all_items = all_items + skipped_items

             all_items, resolved_options.plan, resolved_options.cluster,

     ```
     - Removed collapse_clusters block (moved to callers)

   - **`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/plan_order.py`** — Decomposed from one 100-line function into focused steps
     - `_enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)` — stamps plan description, note, cluster info
     - `_stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids)` — stamps `_plan_position` and `_is_new` on each item
     - `_separate_skipped(items, plan)` — separates skipped items from main list, returns `(non_skipped, skipped)`
     - `_filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, cluster)` — filters to cluster members when focus is active
     - `_stamp_positions(items, plan)` — stamps `queue_position` and `plan_skipped` metadata
     - `apply_plan_order()` — thin wrapper calling the above steps, kept for backward compat
     - `collapse_clusters()` and `_build_cluster_meta()` — unchanged

   - **`desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`** — Modified sort key for unified ordering
     - Extracted `_natural_sort_key(item)` — the original natural ranking logic
     - Rewrote `item_sort_key(item)`:
     ```python
     def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
         plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
         if plan_pos is not None:
             kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
             if kind == "workflow_stage":
                 blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
                 stage_idx = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
                 return (0, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
             return (0, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
         is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
         group = 2 if is_new else 1
         return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
     ```

    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    count: int | None = 1
    59→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    60→    scope: str | None = None
    61→    status: str = "open"

    73→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    74→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""

    82→def build_work_queue(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    86→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    87→    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.
    88→
    89→    Pipeline
    90→    --------
    91→    1. **Build finding items** — filter by status/scope/chronic, apply
    92→       per-detector standalone_threshold (evidence-only gating).
    93→    2. **Subjective items** — build synthetic dimension items, gate
    94→       non-initial ones behind SubjectiveVisibility policy (unless
    95→       the plan explicitly queued them).
    96→    3. **Workflow items** — inject triage stages, score checkpoint,
    97→       and create-plan items when a plan is active.
    98→    4. **Impact enrichment** — estimate per-item score impact from
    99→       dimension_scores; drop mechanical findings below impact floor.
   100→    5. **Plan ordering** — enrich with plan metadata, stamp sort keys
   101→       (``_plan_position`` / ``_is_new``), separate skipped items.
   102→    6. **Sort** — single ``item_sort_key`` pass: planned items by
   103→       position, then existing by natural rank, then new items.
   104→    7. **Post-sort** — re-append skipped if requested, stamp
   105→       ``queue_position``, filter to active cluster focus.
   106→    8. **Count limit** — truncate to ``options.count``.
   107→
   108→    Callers needing collapsed cluster display should call
   109→    ``collapse_clusters(items, plan)`` on the returned items.
   110→    """
   111→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   112→
   113→    # When a QueueContext is provided, its plan and policy take precedence
   114→    # over individually-set fields — the context is the single source of truth.
   115→    # Callers should not set both context and plan/policy on the same options.
   116→    ctx = resolved_options.context
   117→    effective_plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else resolved_options.plan
   118→
   119→    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
   120→    scan_path: str | None = (
   121→        state.get("scan_path")
   122→        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   123→        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    status = resolved_options.status
   127→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   129→    try:
   130→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   131→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   132→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   133→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   134→
   135→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   136→        state,
   137→        scan_path=scan_path,
   138→        status_filter=status,
   139→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   140→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   144→
   145→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
   146→    # QueueContext.policy is pre-computed with correct target_strict/plan;
   147→    # fall back to resolved_options.policy or on-the-fly computation.
   148→    policy = (
   149→        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
   150→        or resolved_options.policy
   151→        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=effective_plan)
   152→    )
   153→
   154→    if (
   155→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   156→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   157→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   158→    ):
   159→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   160→            state,
   161→            state.get("findings", {}),
   162→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   163→        )
   164→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   165→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   166→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   167→            set(effective_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   168→            if effective_plan
   169→            else set()
   170→        )
   171→        for item in subjective_items:
   172→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   173→                continue
   174→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   175→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   176→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   177→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   178→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   179→                continue
   180→            all_items.append(item)
   181→
   182→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   183→    if effective_plan and status in {"open", "all"}:

   195→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(effective_plan)
   196→        if plan_item is not None:
   197→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   198→
   199→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   200→
   201→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   202→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   203→    all_items = [
   204→        item for item in all_items
   205→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   206→        or item.get("is_review")
   207→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   208→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   209→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   210→    ]
   211→
   212→    # Plan-aware pipeline: enrich, stamp sort keys, separate skipped,
   213→    # then a single sort produces the correct ordering.
   214→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   215→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   216→    if effective_plan:
   217→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   218→        _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, effective_plan)
   219→        _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, effective_plan, new_ids)
   220→        all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
   221→            all_items, effective_plan,
   222→        )
   223→
   224→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   225→
   226→    if effective_plan:
   227→        if resolved_options.include_skipped:
   228→            all_items = all_items + skipped_items
   229→        _stamp_positions(all_items, effective_plan)
   230→        all_items = _filter_cluster_focus(
   231→            all_items, effective_plan, resolved_options.cluster,
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    # Inject "run scan" workflow item when queue is empty and a plan cycle
   235→    # is active (plan_start_scores exist).  This gives the user an explicit
   236→    # next-step rather than a bare "nothing to do" message.
   237→    if not all_items and effective_plan:
   238→        plan_scores = effective_plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   239→        if plan_scores.get("strict") is not None:
   240→            all_items.append({
   241→                "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   242→                "kind": "workflow_action",
   243→                "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   244→                "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   245→                "file": "",
   246→                "detector": "workflow",
   247→                "confidence": "high",
   248→            })
   249→
   250→    total = len(all_items)
   251→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   252→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   253→
   254→    if resolved_options.explain:
   255→        for item in all_items:
   256→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   257→
   258→    return {
   259→        "items": all_items,
   260→        "total": total,
   261→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   262→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   263→    }
   264→
   265→
   266→__all__ = [
   267→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   268→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   269→    "QueueContext",
   270→    "WorkQueueResult",
   271→    "build_work_queue",
   272→    "collapse_clusters",
   273→    "group_queue_items",
   274→]
   275→

1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def _enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    25→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    26→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    27→
    28→    for item in items:
    29→        override = overrides.get(item["id"], {})
    30→        if override.get("description"):
    31→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    32→        if override.get("note"):
    33→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    34→        if override.get("cluster"):
    35→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    36→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    37→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    38→                "name": cluster_name,
    39→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    40→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    41→            }
    42→
    43→
    44→def _stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    45→    items: list[dict],
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    new_ids: set[str],
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    50→
    51→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    52→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    53→    """
    54→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    55→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    56→
    57→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    58→    for idx, finding_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    59→        if finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    60→            position_map[finding_id] = idx
    61→
    62→    for item in items:
    63→        item_id = item["id"]
    64→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    65→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    66→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    67→
    68→
    69→def _separate_skipped(
    70→    items: list[dict],
    71→    plan: dict,
    72→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
    73→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    74→
    75→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    76→    """
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→    if not skipped_ids:
    79→        return items, []
    80→    non_skipped: list[dict] = []
    81→    skipped: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    84→            skipped.append(item)
    85→        else:
    86→            non_skipped.append(item)
    87→    return non_skipped, skipped
    88→
    89→
    90→def _filter_cluster_focus(
    91→    items: list[dict],
    92→    plan: dict,
    93→    cluster: str | None,
    94→) -> list[dict]:
    95→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
    97→    if not effective_cluster:
    98→        return items
    99→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   100→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   101→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   102→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   103→        return items
   104→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   105→
   106→
   107→def _stamp_positions(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   109→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   110→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   111→
   112→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   113→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   114→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   115→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   116→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   117→            if skip_entry:
   118→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   119→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   120→                if skip_reason:
   121→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   122→
   123→
   124→def apply_plan_order(
   125→    items: list[dict],
   126→    plan: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   129→    cluster: str | None = None,
   130→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   131→) -> list[dict]:
   132→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   133→
   134→    Thin wrapper that calls the decomposed pipeline steps in order.
   135→    Uses sort-key stamping so a single :func:`item_sort_key` pass
   136→    produces the correct ordering.
   137→    """
   138→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
   139→
   140→    _enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   141→    _stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids or set())
   142→    result, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(items, plan)
   143→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   144→    if include_skipped:
   145→        result = result + skipped_items
   146→    _stamp_positions(result, plan)
   147→    result = _filter_cluster_focus(result, plan, cluster)
   148→    return result
   149→
   150→
   151→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   152→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   153→    try:
   154→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   155→
   156→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→        if meta:
   158→            return meta.action_type
   159→    except ImportError as exc:
   160→        _ = exc
   161→    return "manual_fix"
   162→
   163→
   164→def _build_cluster_meta(
   165→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   166→) -> dict:
   167→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   168→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   169→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   170→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   171→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   172→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   173→        action_type = "reorganize"
   174→    else:
   175→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   176→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   177→            action_type = "refactor"
   178→
   179→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   180→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   181→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   182→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   183→    else:
   184→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   185→
   186→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   187→    if not primary_command:
   188→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   189→
   190→    estimated_impact = max(
   191→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   192→    )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "id": cluster_name,
   196→        "kind": "cluster",
   197→        "action_type": action_type,
   198→        "summary": summary,
   199→        "members": members,
   200→        "member_count": len(members),
   201→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   202→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   203→        "cluster_auto": True,
   204→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   205→        "confidence": "high",
   206→        "detector": detector,
   207→        "file": "",
   208→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   213→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   214→
   215→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   216→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   217→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   218→    """
   219→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   220→    if not clusters:
   221→        return items
   222→
   223→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   224→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   225→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   226→            continue
   227→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   228→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   229→
   230→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   231→        return items
   232→
   233→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   234→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   235→    for item in items:
   236→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   237→        if cname:
   238→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   239→
   240→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   241→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   242→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   243→        if len(members) < 2:
   244→            continue
   245→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   246→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   247→        )
   248→
   249→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   250→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   251→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   252→    result: list[dict] = []
   253→    for item in items:
   254→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   255→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   256→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   257→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   258→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   259→            # skip individual member
   260→        else:
   261→            result.append(item)
   262→    return result
   263→
   264→
   265→__all__ = [
   266→    "_enrich_plan_metadata",
   267→    "_filter_cluster_focus",
   268→    "_separate_skipped",
   269→    "_stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   270→    "_stamp_positions",
   271→    "apply_plan_order",
   272→    "collapse_clusters",
   273→    "new_item_ids",
   274→]
   275→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    81→
    82→    return 0.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    86→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    88→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    89→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    90→
    91→
    92→def build_finding_items(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    *,
    95→    scan_path: str | None,
    96→    status_filter: str,
    97→    scope: str | None,
    98→    chronic: bool,
    99→) -> list[dict]:
   100→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   101→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   102→    out: list[dict] = []
   103→
   104→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   105→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   106→            continue
   107→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   108→            continue
   109→        if chronic and not (
   110→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   111→        ):
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   115→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   116→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   117→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   118→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   119→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   120→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   121→                continue
   122→
   123→        item = dict(finding)
   124→        item["id"] = finding_id
   125→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   126→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   127→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   128→        item["review_weight"] = (
   129→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   130→        )
   131→        subjective_score = None
   132→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   133→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   134→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   135→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   136→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   137→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   138→            )
   139→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   140→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   141→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   142→            item,
   143→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   144→        )
   145→
   146→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   147→            continue
   148→        out.append(item)
   149→
   150→    return out
   151→
   152→
   153→def _natural_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   154→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   155→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   156→
   157→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   158→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   159→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   162→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   163→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   164→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   165→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   166→
   167→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   168→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   169→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   170→
   171→    if kind == "cluster":
   172→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   173→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   174→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   175→        )
   176→        return (
   177→            0,
   178→            action_pri,
   179→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   180→            item.get("id", ""),
   181→        )
   182→
   183→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   184→
   185→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   186→        return (
   187→            1,
   188→            -impact,
   189→            subjective_score_value(item),
   190→            item.get("id", ""),
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   194→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   195→    return (
   196→        1,
   197→        -impact,
   198→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   199→        -review_weight,
   200→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   201→        item.get("id", ""),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→
   205→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   206→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   207→
   208→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`_stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   209→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   210→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   211→
   212→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   213→    """
   214→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   215→
   216→    if plan_pos is not None:
   217→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   218→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   219→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   220→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   221→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   222→            stage_idx = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   223→            return (0, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   224→        return (0, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   225→
   226→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   227→    group = 2 if is_new else 1
   228→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   229→
   230→
   231→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   232→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   233→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   234→        return {
   235→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   236→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   237→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   238→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   239→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   240→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   241→        }
   242→
   243→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   244→        return {
   245→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   246→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after triage stages, before findings.",
   247→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   248→        }
   249→
   250→    if kind == "cluster":
   251→        return {
   252→            "kind": "cluster",
   253→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   254→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   255→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   256→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   257→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   258→        }
   259→
   260→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   261→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   262→        return {
   263→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   264→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   265→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   266→            "initial_review": initial,
   267→            "policy": (
   268→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   269→                if initial else
   270→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   271→            ),
   272→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   273→        }
   274→
   275→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   276→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   277→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   278→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   279→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   280→    if is_subjective:
   281→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   282→    elif is_review:
   283→        ranking_factors = [
   284→            "estimated_impact desc",
   285→            "confidence asc",
   286→            "review_weight desc",
   287→            "count desc",
   288→            "id asc",
   289→        ]
   290→    else:
   291→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   292→    explain = {
   293→        "kind": "finding",
   294→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   295→        "confidence": confidence,
   296→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   297→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   298→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   299→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   300→    }
   301→    if is_review:
   302→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   303→    if is_subjective:
   304→        explain["policy"] = (
   305→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   306→        )
   307→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   308→    return explain
   309→
   310→
   311→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   312→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   313→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   314→    for item in items:
   315→        if group == "file":
   316→            key = item.get("file", "")
   317→        elif group == "detector":
   318→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   319→        elif group == "cluster":
   320→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   321→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   322→        else:
   323→            key = "items"
   324→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   325→    return grouped
   326→
   327→
   328→__all__ = [
   329→    "build_finding_items",
   330→    "enrich_with_impact",
   331→    "item_explain",
   332→    "item_sort_key",
   333→    "subjective_score_value",
   334→    "group_queue_items",
   335→]
   336→
```

1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    11→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    12→)
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    23→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    42→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    47→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    48→        return True
    49→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    50→        members = item.get("members", [])
    51→        return bool(members) and all(
    52→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    53→        )
    54→    return False
    55→
    56→
    57→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    58→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    59→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    60→    weight_by_confidence = {
    61→        "high": 1.0,
    62→        "medium": 0.7,
    63→        "low": 0.3,
    64→    }
    65→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    66→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    67→        weight *= 10.0
    68→    return float(weight)
    69→
    70→
    71→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    72→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    73→    if not scope:
    74→        return True
    75→
    76→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    77→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    78→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    79→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    80→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    81→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    82→
    83→    if "*" in scope:
    84→        return any(
    85→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    86→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    if "::" in scope:
    90→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    91→
    92→    lowered = scope.lower()
    93→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    94→        return (
    95→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    96→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    97→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   101→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   102→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   103→
   104→    return (
   105→        detector == scope
   106→        or filepath == scope
   107→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   108→    )
   109→
   110→
   111→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   112→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   113→
   114→
   115→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   116→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   117→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   118→    if not lang:
   119→        return None
   120→
   121→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   122→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   123→        return None
   124→
   125→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   126→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   127→        return None
   128→
   129→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   130→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   131→        return None
   132→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   133→
   134→
   135→def primary_command_for_finding(
   136→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   137→) -> str:
   138→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   139→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   140→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   141→        available_fixers = [
   142→            fixer
   143→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   144→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   145→        ]
   146→        if available_fixers:
   147→            return f"desloppify autofix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   148→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   149→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   150→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   151→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   152→    return f'desloppify plan resolve "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = [
   156→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   157→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   158→    "detail_dict",
   159→    "is_review_finding",
   160→    "is_subjective_finding",
   161→    "is_subjective_queue_item",
   162→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   163→    "review_finding_weight",
   164→    "scope_matches",
   165→    "slugify",
   166→    "status_matches",
   167→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   168→]
   169→

1→"""Synthetic work-queue item builders and dimension scoring.
     2→
     3→Builds workflow stage items, score checkpoint items, create-plan items,
     4→subjective dimension items, and subjective score lookups.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations

    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Dimension key normalization
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
    29→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
    30→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    31→    target = cleaned.lower()
    32→
    33→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    34→        if str(label).lower() == target:
    35→            return str(dim_key)
    36→    return slugify(cleaned)
    37→
    38→
    39→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
    40→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
    41→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    42→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
    43→    return {
    44→        cleaned.lower(),
    45→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
    46→        slugify(cleaned),
    47→        canonical.lower(),
    48→        slugify(canonical),
    49→    }
    50→
    51→

    53→# Subjective strict scores

    56→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    57→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    58→    if not dim_scores:

    60→
    61→    entries = all_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    62→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    63→    for entry in entries:
    64→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    65→        if not name:
    66→            continue
    67→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    68→        [REDACTED](name)
    69→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
    70→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
    71→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
    72→            if not key:
    73→                continue
    74→            aliases.add(key)
    75→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
    76→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
    77→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
    78→        for alias in aliases:
    79→            scores[alias] = strict_val
    80→    return scores
    81→

    83→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    84→# Synthetic item builders

    86→
    87→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    88→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
    89→
    90→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
    91→    """
    92→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
    93→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    94→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    95→    )
    96→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    97→        TRIAGE_IDS,
    98→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    99→    )
   100→
   101→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   102→    order_set = set(order)
   103→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   104→    if not present:
   105→        return []
   106→
   107→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   108→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   109→
   110→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   111→    open_review_count = sum(
   112→        1 for f in findings.values()
   113→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   114→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   115→    )
   116→
   117→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   118→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   119→
   120→    items: list[dict] = []
   121→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   122→        if sid not in present:
   123→            continue
   124→        if name in confirmed:
   125→            continue
   126→
   127→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   128→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   129→        blocked_by = sorted(
   130→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   131→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   132→        )
   133→
   134→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   135→        if name == "commit":
   136→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   137→
   138→        items.append({
   139→            "id": sid,
   140→            "tier": 1,
   141→            "confidence": "high",
   142→            "detector": "triage",
   143→            "file": ".",
   144→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   145→            "stage_name": name,
   146→            "stage_index": idx,
   147→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   148→            "detail": {
   149→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   150→                "stage": name,
   151→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   152→            },
   153→            "primary_command": cmd,
   154→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   155→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   156→        })
   157→    return items
   158→

   160→def build_score_checkpoint_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:

   162→
   163→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.

   165→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID

   167→    if WORKFLOW_SCORE_CHECKPOINT_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   168→        return None
   169→
   170→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   171→
   172→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   173→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   174→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   175→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   176→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""

   178→    return {

   277→            if str(key).strip()
   278→        ]
   279→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   280→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   281→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   282→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   283→            name in unassessed_dims
   284→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   285→        )
   286→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   287→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   288→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   289→        if open_review > 0:
   290→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   291→        else:
   292→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   293→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   294→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   295→        items.append(
   296→            {
   297→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   298→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   299→                "file": ".",
   300→                "confidence": "medium",
   301→                "summary": summary,
   302→                "detail": {
   303→                    "dimension_name": name,
   304→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   305→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   306→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   307→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   308→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   309→                },
   310→                "status": "open",
   311→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   312→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   313→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   314→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   315→            }
   316→        )
   317→    return items
   318→
   319→
   320→def build_import_scores_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   321→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::import-scores`` if it's in the queue.
   322→
   323→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   324→    """
   325→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID
   326→
   327→    if WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   328→        return None
   329→
   330→    return {
   331→        "id": WORKFLOW_IMPORT_SCORES_ID,
   332→        "tier": 1,
   333→        "confidence": "high",
   334→        "detector": "workflow",
   335→        "file": ".",
   336→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   337→        "summary": "Import assessment scores with attestation",
   338→        "detail": {
   339→            "explanation": (
   340→                "Review findings were imported but assessment scores were skipped "
   341→                "(untrusted source). Re-import with attestation to update dimension scores."
   342→            ),
   343→        },
   344→        "primary_command": (
   345→            'desloppify review --import findings.json --attested-external '
   346→            '--attest "I validated this review was completed without awareness '
   347→            'of overall score and is unbiased."'
   348→        ),
   349→        "blocked_by": [],
   350→        "is_blocked": False,
   351→    }
   352→
   353→
   354→def build_communicate_score_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   355→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::communicate-score`` if it's in the queue.
   356→
   357→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   358→    """
   359→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID
   360→
   361→    if WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   362→        return None
   363→
   364→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   365→
   366→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   367→    strict = snapshot.strict if snapshot.strict is not None else 0.0
   368→    plan_start = (plan.get("plan_start_scores") or {}).get("strict")
   369→    delta = round(strict - plan_start, 1) if plan_start is not None else None
   370→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta else ""
   371→
   372→    return {
   373→        "id": WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID,
   374→        "tier": 1,
   375→        "confidence": "high",
   376→        "detector": "workflow",
   377→        "file": ".",
   378→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   379→        "summary": f"Communicate updated score to user: strict {strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}",
   380→        "detail": {
   381→            "strict": strict,
   382→            "plan_start_strict": plan_start,
   383→            "delta": delta,
   384→        },
   385→        "primary_command": (
   386→            f'desloppify plan resolve "{WORKFLOW_COMMUNICATE_SCORE_ID}" '
   387→            '--note "Score communicated" --confirm'
   388→        ),
   389→        "blocked_by": [],
   390→        "is_blocked": False,
   391→    }
   392→
   393→
   394→__all__ = [
   395→    "build_communicate_score_item",
   396→    "build_create_plan_item",

   398→    "build_score_checkpoint_item",
   399→    "build_subjective_items",
   400→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   401→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   402→]
   403→

1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    14→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    15→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    20→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = object()
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class QueueContext:
    25→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    26→
    27→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    28→    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    29→    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    30→    """
    31→
    32→    plan: dict | None
    33→    target_strict: float
    34→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    35→
    36→
    37→def queue_context(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    config: dict | None = None,
    41→    plan: dict | None | object = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    42→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    43→) -> QueueContext:
    44→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    45→
    46→    Resolution order:
    47→
    48→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    49→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    50→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
    51→       via ``target_strict_score_from_config``; final fallback is ``95.0``.
    52→    3. **policy** — ``compute_subjective_visibility(state, ...)`` with the
    53→       resolved plan and target_strict so every downstream consumer sees
    54→       the same objective-vs-subjective balance.
    55→    """
    56→    # --- resolve plan ---
    57→    if plan is _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD:
    58→        from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    59→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    60→
    61→        try:
    62→            resolved_plan: dict | None = load_plan()
    63→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    64→            resolved_plan = None
    65→    else:
    66→        resolved_plan = plan  # type: ignore[assignment]
    67→
    68→    # --- resolve target_strict ---
    69→    if target_strict is not None:
    70→        resolved_target = target_strict
    71→    elif config is not None:
    72→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import (
    73→            target_strict_score_from_config,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        resolved_target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    77→    else:
    78→        resolved_target = 95.0
    79→
    80→    # --- resolve policy ---
    81→    resolved_policy = compute_subjective_visibility(
    82→        state,
    83→        target_strict=resolved_target,
    84→        plan=resolved_plan,
    85→    )
    86→
    87→    return QueueContext(
    88→        plan=resolved_plan,
    89→        target_strict=resolved_target,
    90→        policy=resolved_policy,
    91→    )
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = [
    95→    "QueueContext",
    96→    "queue_context",
    97→]
    98→
```

Perfect! I now have all the files. Let me provide you with a comprehensive analysis of the work queue pipeline.

## Work Queue Pipeline - Complete Analysis

### File Summary

| File | Lines | Status |
|------|-------|--------|
| **core.py** | 275 | Main orchestrator - coordinates all pipeline stages |
| **plan_order.py** | 275 | Plan-aware ordering, cluster collapsing, item enrichment |
| **ranking.py** | 336 | Impact calculation, sort keys, natural ranking |
| **helpers.py** | 169 | Predicates, utilities, item classification |
| **synthetic.py** | 403 | Builds synthetic workflow/triage/subjective items |
| **context.py** | 98 | Unified context object for queue resolution |
| **work_queue.py** (facade) | 95 | Public API re-exports (no implementation) |

---

## Architecture Overview

The work queue pipeline follows a **functional, decomposed design** where each module has a specific responsibility. The flow is:

```
build_work_queue() [core.py]
  ↓
1. build_finding_items() [ranking.py] — filter & create base items
  ↓
2. build_subjective_items() [synthetic.py] — synthetic dimension items
  ↓
3. Workflow items (triage stages, checkpoints, plan items) [synthetic.py]
  ↓
4. enrich_with_impact() [ranking.py] — score impact from dimensions
  ↓
5. Plan pipeline (if active):
   - _enrich_plan_metadata() [plan_order.py]
   - _stamp_plan_sort_keys() [plan_order.py]
   - _separate_skipped() [plan_order.py]
  ↓
6. item_sort_key() [ranking.py] — single unified sort pass
  ↓
7. Post-sort adjustments:
   - _stamp_positions() [plan_order.py]
   - _filter_cluster_focus() [plan_order.py]
  ↓
8. Count limit & explain
  ↓
collapse_clusters() [plan_order.py] — optional collapsed display
```

---

## Module-by-Module Details

### 1. **core.py** (275 lines)
**Purpose:** Main entry point that orchestrates the entire pipeline.

**Key Types:**
- `QueueBuildOptions(frozen=True)` — Configuration dataclass with fields:
  - `count`, `scan_path`, `scope`, `status`, `include_subjective`
  - `subjective_threshold`, `chronic`, `explain`, `plan`, `include_skipped`, `cluster`
  - `policy`, `context` (immutable queue context)
- `WorkQueueResult(TypedDict)` — Return type:
  - `items: list[dict]` — final queue items
  - `total: int` — count before truncation
  - `grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]` — grouped by "item" field
  - `new_ids: set[str]` — findings added in most recent scan

**Core Function:**
```python
build_work_queue(state: StateModel, *, options: QueueBuildOptions | None) → WorkQueueResult
```

**Pipeline Steps (in order):**
1. **Finding items** — call `build_finding_items()` with status/scope/chronic filters
2. **Subjective items** — call `build_subjective_items()`, gate via `SubjectiveVisibility` policy
3. **Workflow items** — inject triage stages, checkpoints, import-scores, communicate-score, create-plan
4. **Impact enrichment** — call `enrich_with_impact()` with dimension_scores
5. **Impact floor** — drop mechanical findings with < 0.05 impact
6. **Plan pipeline** — if plan active:
   - Get new_ids via `_new_item_ids()`
   - Enrich plan metadata
   - Stamp sort keys + is_new flags
   - Separate skipped items
7. **Single sort** — `all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)`
8. **Post-sort** — re-append skipped if requested, stamp positions, filter cluster focus
9. **Count limit** — truncate to `options.count`
10. **Explain** — optionally stamp `item["explain"]`

**Imports from siblings:**
- `ranking.py`: `build_finding_items`, `enrich_with_impact`, `group_queue_items`, `item_explain`, `item_sort_key`
- `plan_order.py`: `_enrich_plan_metadata`, `_filter_cluster_focus`, `_separate_skipped`, `_stamp_plan_sort_keys`, `_stamp_positions`, `collapse_clusters`, `new_item_ids`
- `synthetic.py`: `build_*_item()` functions (6 builders)
- `context.py`: `QueueContext`
- `helpers.py`: `ALL_STATUSES`, `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`, `scope_matches`

**Exports:** `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`, `QueueBuildOptions`, `QueueContext`, `WorkQueueResult`, `build_work_queue`, `collapse_clusters`, `group_queue_items`

---

### 2. **plan_order.py** (275 lines)
**Purpose:** Plan-aware ordering and cluster collapsing; decomposes the plan pipeline into testable stages.

**Key Functions:**

1. **`new_item_ids(state) → set[str]`**
   - Returns finding IDs added in the most recent scan
   - Compares `first_seen` timestamp against scan_history[-1].timestamp

2. **`_enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan) → None`**
   - Stamps `plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster` from overrides and clusters
   - Modifies items in-place

3. **`_stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids) → None`**
   - Stamps `_plan_position` (index in queue_order) and `_is_new` on each item
   - Used by `item_sort_key()` for unified ordering
   - Skipped items get `_plan_position = None`

4. **`_separate_skipped(items, plan) → tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]`**
   - Returns `(non_skipped, skipped)` for optional re-append

5. **`_filter_cluster_focus(items, plan, cluster) → list[dict]`**
   - Filters to cluster members when active_cluster or explicit cluster param is set
   - No-op if no cluster focus active

6. **`_stamp_positions(items, plan) → None`**
   - Stamps `queue_position` (1-indexed) and skip metadata (`plan_skipped`, `plan_skip_kind`, `plan_skip_reason`)
   - Operates on final sorted list

7. **`apply_plan_order(items, plan, *, include_skipped, cluster, new_ids) → list[dict]`**
   - Thin wrapper orchestrating the above steps in sequence
   - Re-imports `item_sort_key` to avoid circular dependency

8. **`collapse_clusters(items, plan) → list[dict]`**
   - Replaces cluster member items with single meta-items
   - Only collapses clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   - First member replaced with meta-item, others skipped
   - Calls `_build_cluster_meta()` internally to construct meta-items

**Cluster Meta-Item Fields:**
- `id`, `kind: "cluster"`, `action_type`, `summary`, `members`, `member_count`
- `primary_command`, `cluster_name`, `cluster_auto: bool`, `cluster_optional: bool`
- `confidence`, `detector`, `file`, `estimated_impact`

**Imports from siblings:**
- `ranking.py`: `item_sort_key` (lazy import to avoid cycles)
- `registry.py`: `DETECTORS` (for action_type_for_detector)

**Exports:** `_enrich_plan_metadata`, `_filter_cluster_focus`, `_separate_skipped`, `_stamp_plan_sort_keys`, `_stamp_positions`, `apply_plan_order`, `collapse_clusters`, `new_item_ids`

---

### 3. **ranking.py** (336 lines)
**Purpose:** Impact calculation, ranking logic, and sort key generation.

**Key Functions:**

1. **`enrich_with_impact(items, dimension_scores) → None`**
   - Computes `estimated_impact = overall_per_point × headroom` for each item
   - Headroom = 100 - dimension_score
   - For subjective items: looks up dimension by detail.dimension_name
   - For mechanical findings: uses get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   - Stamps `estimated_impact` in-place

2. **`build_finding_items(state, *, scan_path, status_filter, scope, chronic) → list[dict]`**
   - Core item builder from findings dict
   - Filters by:
     - status (via status_matches)
     - scope (via scope_matches)
     - chronic (reopen_count >= 2)
     - **standalone_threshold** — gating for evidence-only mechanical findings
   - Enriches each finding:
     - `id`, `kind: "finding"`, `is_review`, `is_subjective`, `review_weight`
     - `subjective_score` (for subjective items)
     - `primary_command` (from helpers)

3. **`_natural_sort_key(item) → tuple`**
   - Computes natural (plan-unaware) ranking
   - **Tier -3**: Initial-review subjective items (priority order by score)
   - **Tier -2**: Workflow stage items (by blocked penalty, stage_index)
   - **Tier -1**: Workflow action items
   - **Tier 0**: Clusters (by action_type, member_count, id)
   - **Tier 1**: Subjective/regular findings (by impact desc, confidence asc, count desc)

4. **`item_sort_key(item) → tuple`**
   - **Plan-aware unified sort key**
   - If `_plan_position` is set: `(0, 0, plan_pos, id)` — planned items first
   - Otherwise: `(group, *_natural_sort_key)` where group ∈ {1 (existing), 2 (new)}
   - Special: triage stages maintain blocked-before-unblocked invariant even if planned

5. **`subjective_score_value(item) → float`**
   - Returns strict_score for subjective_dimension kinds, else subjective_score field

6. **`item_explain(item) → dict`**
   - Inspection helper returning ranking factors and policy for each item kind
   - Used with `explain: bool` option in queue building

7. **`group_queue_items(items, group: str) → dict[str, list[dict]]`**
   - Groups by "file", "detector", "cluster", or default "items"

**Imports from siblings:**
- `helpers.py`: `ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY`, `detail_dict`, `is_review_finding`, `is_subjective_finding`, `primary_command_for_finding`, `review_finding_weight`, `scope_matches`, `slugify`, `status_matches`, `supported_fixers_for_item`
- `synthetic.py`: `subjective_strict_scores`
- `registry.py`: `DETECTORS`
- `planning/common.py`: `CONFIDENCE_ORDER`

**Exports:** `build_finding_items`, `enrich_with_impact`, `item_explain`, `item_sort_key`, `subjective_score_value`, `group_queue_items`

---

### 4. **helpers.py** (169 lines)
**Purpose:** Small predicates and utilities for item classification and field extraction.

**Constants:**
- `ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))`
- `ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}`
- `ATTEST_EXAMPLE` — boilerplate text for attestations

**Key Functions:**

1. **`detail_dict(item) → dict`**
   - Safe accessor for `item["detail"]`; returns {} if not a dict

2. **`status_matches(item_status, status_filter) → bool`**
   - True if filter == "all" or status == filter

3. **`is_subjective_finding(item) → bool`**
   - True for detectors in {"subjective_assessment", "holistic_review"}

4. **`is_review_finding(item) → bool`**
   - True if detector == "review"

5. **`is_subjective_queue_item(item) → bool`**
   - True for subjective_dimension kinds or clusters of all-subjective members

6. **`review_finding_weight(item) → float`**
   - Returns 0.3, 0.7, or 1.0 based on confidence
   - Multiplied by 10× if holistic

7. **`scope_matches(item, scope: str | None) → bool`**
   - Pattern matching against item_id, detector, filepath, summary, dimension
   - Supports: glob patterns, `::` prefix matching, 8+ hex hash suffix, direct equality

8. **`slugify(text) → str`**
   - Normalizes to lowercase alphanumeric + underscore

9. **`supported_fixers_for_item(state, item) → set[str] | None`**
   - Looks up language capabilities from state for item's lang

10. **`primary_command_for_finding(item, *, supported_fixers) → str`**
    - Returns CLI command string for item resolution
    - Prioritizes autofix if available, else subjective_review, else plan resolve

**Imports:**
- `registry.py`: `DETECTORS`
- `integrity.py`: `is_holistic_subjective_finding`

**Exports:** `ALL_STATUSES`, `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`, `detail_dict`, `is_review_finding`, `is_subjective_finding`, `is_subjective_queue_item`, `primary_command_for_finding`, `review_finding_weight`, `scope_matches`, `slugify`, `status_matches`, `supported_fixers_for_item`

---

### 5. **synthetic.py** (403 lines)
**Purpose:** Builders for synthetic work queue items (workflow stages, checkpoints, subjective dimensions).

**Dimension Key Normalization:**

1. **`_canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name) → str`**
   - Maps display labels (e.g., "Mid elegance") to canonical dimension key
   - Strips " (subjective)" suffix, looks up in DISPLAY_NAMES

2. **`_subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name) → set[str]`**
   - Returns normalized aliases for matching

**Subjective Scoring:**

3. **`subjective_strict_scores(state) → dict[str, float]`**
   - Builds dimension key → strict score lookup from dimension_scores
   - Uses `all_subjective_entries()` to get subjective dimension list
   - Creates multiple aliases per dimension for flexible matching

**Synthetic Item Builders:**

4. **`build_triage_stage_items(plan, state) → list[dict]`**
   - Creates workflow_stage items for pending triage::* IDs
   - Stages: observe → reflect → organize → commit
   - Computes `blocked_by` from TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES
   - Stamps `is_blocked`, `stage_index`, `stage_name`
   - Command: `desloppify plan triage --stage {name}` (or `--complete` for commit)

5. **`build_score_checkpoint_item(plan, state) → dict | None`**
   - Creates workflow_action for workflow::score-checkpoint if in queue_order
   - Shows strict score snapshot with delta vs plan_start_scores

6. **`build_create_plan_item(plan) → dict | None`**
   - Creates workflow_action for workflow::create-plan if in queue_order

7. **`build_import_scores_item(plan, state) → dict | None`**
   - Creates workflow_action for workflow::import-scores if in queue_order
   - Indicates need to re-import with attestation

8. **`build_communicate_score_item(plan, state) → dict | None`**
   - Creates workflow_action for workflow::communicate-score if in queue_order
   - Shows final score delta

9. **`build_subjective_items(state, findings, *, threshold) → list[dict]`**
   - Creates subjective_dimension items for dimension scores below threshold
   - Enriches with:
     - `is_unassessed`: placeholder or stale or 0 score with 0 issues
     - `is_stale`: marked in dimension_scores
     - `initial_review`: unassessed items (onboarding priority)
     - `stale_review`: stale but assessed
     - Open review finding counts per dimension
   - Commands: `desloppify review --prepare` (default) or `desloppify show review` (if open findings exist)

**Imports:**
- `planning/scorecard_projection.py`: `all_subjective_entries`
- `integrity.py`: `is_holistic_subjective_finding`, `unassessed_subjective_dimensions`
- `scoring.py`: `DISPLAY_NAMES`
- `plan/stale_dimensions.py`: IDs and labels for triage/workflow items

**Exports:** `build_communicate_score_item`, `build_create_plan_item`, `build_import_scores_item`, `build_score_checkpoint_item`, `build_subjective_items`, `build_triage_stage_items`, `subjective_strict_scores`

---

### 6. **context.py** (98 lines)
**Purpose:** Unified context object to thread queue resolution parameters.

**Key Type:**
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueContext:
    plan: dict | None
    target_strict: float
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
```

**Function:**
```python
queue_context(state, *, config=None, plan=_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD, target_strict=None) → QueueContext
```

**Resolution Order:**
1. **plan** — explicit value, or auto-load from disk (guarded by PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS)
2. **target_strict** — explicit float, or from config, or fallback 95.0
3. **policy** — compute_subjective_visibility with resolved plan and target

**Purpose:** Replaces scattered `plan` / `target_strict` / `policy` threading through function chains with a single immutable object.

**Imports:**
- `plan.py`: `load_plan` (lazy)
- `exception_sets.py`: `PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS`
- `helpers/score.py`: `target_strict_score_from_config`
- `_plan/subjective_policy.py`: `SubjectiveVisibility`, `compute_subjective_visibility`

**Exports:** `QueueContext`, `queue_context`

---

### 7. **work_queue.py** (95 lines) — Facade
**Purpose:** Public API re-exports; no implementation logic.

**Re-exports from:**
- `context.py`: `QueueContext`, `queue_context`
- `core.py`: `ATTEST_EXAMPLE`, `QueueBuildOptions`, `WorkQueueResult`, `build_work_queue`, `group_queue_items`
- `helpers.py`: `ALL_STATUSES`, `is_review_finding`, `is_subjective_finding`, `is_subjective_queue_item`, `primary_command_for_finding`, `review_finding_weight`, `scope_matches`, `slugify`, `status_matches`, `supported_fixers_for_item`
- `synthetic.py`: `build_subjective_items`, `subjective_strict_scores`
- `issues.py`: `expire_stale_holistic`, `impact_label`, `list_open_review_findings`, `update_investigation` (NOT YET READ)
- `ranking.py`: `build_finding_items`, `item_explain`, `item_sort_key`, `subjective_score_value`

---

## Cross-Module Import Dependencies

```
core.py
  ├─→ ranking.py (build_finding_items, enrich_with_impact, group_queue_items, item_explain, item_sort_key)
  ├─→ plan_order.py (_enrich_plan_metadata, _filter_cluster_focus, _separate_skipped, _stamp_plan_sort_keys, _stamp_positions, collapse_clusters, new_item_ids)
  ├─→ synthetic.py (6 builders)
  ├─→ context.py (QueueContext)
  └─→ helpers.py (ALL_STATUSES, ATTEST_EXAMPLE, scope_matches)

ranking.py
  ├─→ helpers.py (8 utilities)
  ├─→ synthetic.py (subjective_strict_scores)
  ├─→ registry.py (DETECTORS)
  └─→ state.py (path_scoped_findings)

plan_order.py
  ├─→ ranking.py (item_sort_key — lazy import)
  └─→ registry.py (DETECTORS — lazy import)

synthetic.py
  ├─→ planning/scorecard_projection.py (all_subjective_entries)
  ├─→ integrity.py (is_holistic_subjective_finding, unassessed_subjective_dimensions)
  ├─→ scoring.py (DISPLAY_NAMES)
  ├─→ helpers.py (detail_dict, slugify)
  └─→ _plan/stale_dimensions.py (IDs and labels)

context.py
  ├─→ plan.py (load_plan — lazy)
  ├─→ exception_sets.py (PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS)
  ├─→ helpers/score.py (target_strict_score_from_config)
  └─→ _plan/subjective_policy.py (SubjectiveVisibility, compute_subjective_visibility)

helpers.py
  ├─→ registry.py (DETECTORS)
  └─→ integrity.py (is_holistic_subjective_finding)

work_queue.py (facade)
  └─→ (all of above)
```

---

## Key Design Patterns

1. **Single Sort Pass:** All items stamped with sort metadata (`_plan_position`, `_is_new`) before one unified `item_sort_key()` sort.

2. **In-Place Modifications:** Many functions modify items dict directly for efficiency (e.g., `_enrich_plan_metadata`, `_stamp_plan_sort_keys`).

3. **Lazy Imports:** Avoid circular dependencies by importing late (e.g., `item_sort_key` in plan_order.py, `load_plan` in context.py).

4. **Fallback Hierarchies:** `scope_matches()` supports glob, `::` prefix, hash suffix, and direct equality. `dimension key lookup uses aliases.

5. **Item Classification:** Predicates (`is_review_finding`, `is_subjective_finding`) and `kind` field (finding/cluster/subjective_dimension/workflow_stage/workflow_action) guide branching.

6. **Impact-Aware Sorting:** Items from high-headroom dimensions sort first; same-impact items ordered by natural ranking (confidence, count, etc.).

7. **Frozen Immutable Context:** `QueueContext` is frozen to prevent threading bugs; contains pre-computed `SubjectiveVisibility` policy.

8. **Plan Optionality:** All stages work with or without a plan; plan fields are optional on items and only stamp when active.

   329→    item = queue["items"][0]

   336→def test_queue_build_options_defaults():

   342→    assert opts.status == "open"

   382→# ── Subjective threshold clamping ─────────────────────────
   383→
   384→
   385→def test_subjective_threshold_clamped_to_valid_range():
   386→    """Threshold values outside [0, 100] are clamped, not rejected."""
   387→    state = _state(
   388→        [],
   389→        dimension_scores={
   390→            "Naming quality": {"score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "issues": 1},
   391→        },
   392→    )
   393→    # threshold=-10 clamps to 0.0 -> score 50 >= 0 -> item excluded
   394→    queue = build_work_queue(
   395→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=-10
   396→    )
   397→    subj_items = [item for item in queue["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   398→    assert subj_items == []
   399→
   400→    # threshold=200 clamps to 100.0 -> score 50 < 100 -> item included
   401→    queue2 = build_work_queue(
   402→        state, count=None, include_subjective=True, subjective_threshold=200
   403→    )
   404→    subj_items2 = [item for item in queue2["items"] if item["kind"] == "subjective_dimension"]
   405→    assert len(subj_items2) >= 1
   406→

   534→# ── Subjective items appear alongside mechanical ──────────
