```
   536→
   537→def test_stale_subjective_gated_when_objective_backlog_exists():
   538→    """Stale subjective items are suppressed while objective backlog exists."""
   539→    objective_findings = [
   540→        _finding(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   541→        for c in "abcd"
   542→    ]
   543→    state = _state(
   544→        objective_findings,
   545→        dimension_scores={
   546→            "Naming quality": {
   547→                "score": 70.0,
   548→                "strict": 70.0,
   549→                "issues": 1,
   550→                "detectors": {
   551→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   552→                },
   553→            },
   554→        },
   555→    )
   556→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {
   557→        "naming_quality": {
   558→            "score": 70.0,
   559→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   560→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   561→        }
   562→    }
   563→
   564→    queue = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   565→    ids = [item["id"] for item in queue["items"]]
   566→    subj_ids = [i for i in ids if i.startswith("subjective::")]
   567→
   568→    # Stale subjective items are gated when objective backlog > 0
   569→    assert len(subj_ids) == 0
   570→    assert len(ids) == 4  # 4 objective only
   571→

   642→# ── Impact-based ordering ──────────────────────────────────
   643→
   644→
   645→def test_high_impact_subjective_before_low_impact_mechanical():
   646→    """A subjective dimension with high headroom sorts before a mechanical
   647→    finding in a near-perfect dimension."""
   648→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   649→
   650→    subj_item = {
   651→        "id": "subjective::naming",
   652→        "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   653→        "detail": {"dimension_name": "Naming quality", "strict_score": 43.0},
   654→        "confidence": "medium",
   655→    }
   656→    mech_item = {
   657→        "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   658→        "kind": "finding",
   659→        "detector": "smells",
   660→        "confidence": "high",
   661→        "detail": {"count": 1},
   662→    }
   663→
   664→    dimension_scores = {
   665→        "Naming quality": {
   666→            "score": 43.0,
   667→            "strict": 43.0,
   668→            "issues": 3,
   669→            "detectors": {
   670→                "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   671→            },
   672→        },
   673→        "Code quality": {
   674→            "score": 97.0,
   675→            "strict": 97.0,
   676→            "checks": 200,
   677→            "issues": 1,
   678→            "detectors": {
   679→                "smells": {"weighted_failures": 1, "potential": 200},
   680→            },
   681→        },
   682→    }
   683→
   684→    enrich_with_impact([subj_item, mech_item], dimension_scores)
   685→
   686→    # Naming quality has much more headroom (57) than Code quality (3)
   687→    assert subj_item["estimated_impact"] > mech_item["estimated_impact"]
   688→    assert item_sort_key(subj_item) < item_sort_key(mech_item)
   689→
   690→
   691→def test_impact_fallback_when_no_dimension_scores():
   692→    """When no dimension_scores exist, all items get impact 0.0 and
   693→    tiebreakers provide default ordering."""
   694→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import enrich_with_impact, item_sort_key
   695→
   696→    items = [
   697→        {
   698→            "id": "smells::src/a.py::x",
   699→            "kind": "finding",
   700→            "detector": "smells",
   701→            "confidence": "high",
   702→            "detail": {"count": 5},
   703→        },
   704→        {
   705→            "id": "smells::src/b.py::y",
   706→            "kind": "finding",
   707→            "detector": "smells",
   708→            "confidence": "medium",
   709→            "detail": {"count": 2},
   710→        },
   711→    ]
   712→
   713→    enrich_with_impact(items, {})
   714→
   715→    assert all(item["estimated_impact"] == 0.0 for item in items)
   716→    # High confidence sorts before medium when impact is equal
   717→    assert item_sort_key(items[0]) < item_sort_key(items[1])
   718→

   823→def test_registry_standalone_threshold_count():
   824→    """Exactly 7 detectors have standalone_threshold='medium'."""
   825→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   826→
   827→    threshold_detectors = [

   829→        if meta.standalone_threshold == "medium"

   836→# ── Cluster collapse ─────────────────────────────────────
   837→
   838→
   839→def test_collapse_clusters_preserves_order():
   840→    """Cluster meta-item appears at position of first member, not re-sorted."""
   841→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import collapse_clusters
   842→
   843→    plan: dict = {
   844→        "queue_order": [],
   845→        "skipped": {},
   846→        "overrides": {},
   847→        "clusters": {},
   848→        "active_cluster": None,
   849→    }
   850→    plan["clusters"]["auto/unused"] = {
   851→        "name": "auto/unused",
   852→        "auto": True,
   853→        "cluster_key": "auto::unused",
   854→        "finding_ids": ["u1", "u2"],
   855→        "description": "Remove 2 unused findings",
   856→        "action": "desloppify autofix unused-imports --dry-run",
   857→        "user_modified": False,
   858→    }
   859→
   860→    # Place a non-cluster item first, then the two cluster members
   861→    items = [
   862→        {"id": "other", "kind": "finding", "detector": "structural",
   863→         "confidence": "medium", "detail": {}},
   864→        {"id": "u1", "kind": "finding", "detector": "unused",
   865→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   866→        {"id": "u2", "kind": "finding", "detector": "unused",
   867→         "confidence": "high", "detail": {}, "estimated_impact": 1.0},
   868→    ]
   869→
   870→    result = collapse_clusters(items, plan)
   871→    # Non-cluster item stays at position 0
   872→    assert result[0]["id"] == "other"
   873→    # Cluster meta-item appears at position 1 (where first member was)
   874→    assert result[1]["kind"] == "cluster"
   875→    assert result[1]["id"] == "auto/unused"
   876→    assert len(result) == 2  # other + cluster
   877→
   878→
   879→# -- Plan-ordered subjective items surface despite objective backlog --------
   880→
   881→
   882→def test_plan_ordered_stale_subjective_surfaces_with_objective_backlog():
   883→    """Stale subjective items in plan queue_order surface even with objective backlog.
   884→
   885→    After a completed cycle, stale dims are injected into queue_order by
   886→    sync_stale_dimensions.  The work queue must respect the plan's explicit
   887→    ordering and surface them even though should_surface() would normally
   888→    filter them.
   889→    """
   890→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
   891→
   892→    objective_findings = [
   893→        _finding(f"smells::src/{c}.py::x", detector="smells", tier=3)
   894→        for c in "abcd"
   895→    ]
   896→    state = _state(
   897→        objective_findings,
   898→        dimension_scores={
   899→            "Naming quality": {
   900→                "score": 70.0,
   901→                "strict": 70.0,
   902→                "issues": 1,
   903→                "detectors": {
   904→                    "subjective_assessment": {"dimension_key": "naming_quality"},
   905→                },
   906→            },
   907→        },
   908→    )
   909→    state["subjective_assessments"] = {

   912→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
   913→            "stale_since": "2026-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
   914→        }
   915→    }
   916→
   917→    # Without plan: stale subjective item is gated (existing behavior)
   918→    queue_no_plan = build_work_queue(state, count=None, include_subjective=True)
   919→    subj_no_plan = [
   920→        i["id"] for i in queue_no_plan["items"] if i["id"].startswith("subjective::")
   921→    ]
   922→    assert len(subj_no_plan) == 0
   923→
   924→    # With plan that includes the stale dim in queue_order: surfaces
   925→    plan = empty_plan()
   926→    plan["queue_order"] = [
   927→        "subjective::naming_quality",

   933→    queue_with_plan = build_work_queue(

   938→    ]

   940→    assert "subjective::naming_quality" in subj_with_plan
   941→
   942→    # And it should be first in the queue (plan order respected)
   943→    assert queue_with_plan["items"][0]["id"] == "subjective::naming_quality"

   949→def test_wontfixed_findings_excluded_from_queue():
   950→    """Findings with status 'wontfix' never appear in the default queue.

   953→    auto-generated queue because filtering was spread across multiple
```

```
1→"""Tests for queue order guard in resolve command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import importlib
     6→from unittest.mock import MagicMock

     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan, ensure_plan_defaults

    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# Lazy import helper — avoids the circular import in resolve/render
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→def _import_guard():
    17→    """Import _check_queue_order_guard, patching away the render dependency."""
    18→    import desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd as cmd_mod
    19→    return cmd_mod._check_queue_order_guard
    20→
    21→
    22→def _get_guard_fn():
    23→    """Return the guard function, handling circular import gracefully."""
    24→    try:
    25→        return _import_guard()
    26→    except ImportError:
    27→        # Fall back: patch the problematic render import chain
    28→        import sys
    29→        stub = MagicMock()
    30→        modules_to_stub = [
    31→            "desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update",
    32→            "desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers",
    33→        ]
    34→        saved = {}
    35→        for mod_name in modules_to_stub:
    36→            if mod_name in sys.modules:
    37→                saved[mod_name] = sys.modules[mod_name]
    38→            sys.modules[mod_name] = stub
    39→
    40→        # Force reimport
    41→        import desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd as cmd_mod
    42→        importlib.reload(cmd_mod)
    43→        fn = cmd_mod._check_queue_order_guard
    44→
    45→        # Restore
    46→        for mod_name in modules_to_stub:
    47→            if mod_name in saved:
    48→                sys.modules[mod_name] = saved[mod_name]
    49→            else:
    50→                sys.modules.pop(mod_name, None)
    51→        return fn
    52→
    53→
    54→_check_queue_order_guard = _get_guard_fn()

    84→    if clusters:

    86→    save_plan(plan, plan_file)
```

```
1→"""State-backed work queue for review findings.
     2→
     3→Review findings live in state["findings"]. This module provides:
     4→- Listing/sorting open review findings by impact
     5→- Storing investigation notes on findings
     6→- Expiring stale holistic findings during scan
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→from datetime import UTC, datetime
    13→
    14→from desloppify.core.issues_render import finding_weight
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import detail_dict
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→__all__ = [
    20→    "impact_label",
    21→    "list_open_review_findings",
    22→    "update_investigation",
    23→    "expire_stale_holistic",
    24→]
    25→
    26→
    27→def impact_label(weight: float) -> str:
    28→    """Convert weight to a human-readable impact label."""
    29→    try:
    30→        numeric = float(weight)
    31→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    32→        return "+"
    33→    if numeric >= 8:
    34→        return "+++"
    35→    if numeric >= 5:
    36→        return "++"
    37→    return "+"
    38→
    39→
    40→def list_open_review_findings(state: dict) -> list[dict]:
    41→    """Return open review findings sorted by impact (highest first)."""
    42→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    43→    review = [
    44→        finding
    45→        for finding in findings.values()
    46→        if finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("detector") == "review"
    47→    ]
    48→
    49→    def _sort_key(finding: dict) -> tuple[float, str]:
    50→        weight, _impact, finding_id = finding_weight(finding)
    51→        return (-weight, finding_id)
    52→
    53→    review.sort(key=_sort_key)
    54→    return review
    55→
    56→
    57→def update_investigation(state: dict, finding_id: str, text: str) -> bool:
    58→    """Store investigation text on a finding. Returns False if not found/not open."""
    59→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    60→    if not finding or finding.get("status") != "open":
    61→        return False
    62→    detail = detail_dict(finding)
    63→    if not detail:
    64→        detail = {}
    65→        finding["detail"] = detail
    66→    detail["investigation"] = text
    67→    detail["investigated_at"] = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()
    68→    return True
    69→
    70→
    71→def expire_stale_holistic(state: dict, max_age_days: int = 30) -> list[str]:
    72→    """Auto-resolve holistic review findings older than max_age_days."""
    73→    now = datetime.now(UTC)
    74→    expired: list[str] = []
    75→
    76→    for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    77→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    78→            continue
    79→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    80→            continue
    81→        if not detail_dict(finding).get("holistic"):
    82→            continue
    83→
    84→        last_seen = finding.get("last_seen")
    85→        if not last_seen:
    86→            continue
    87→
    88→        try:
    89→            seen_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_seen)
    90→        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    91→            logger.debug(
    92→                "Skipping holistic finding %s with invalid last_seen %r: %s",
    93→                finding_id,
    94→                last_seen,
    95→                exc,
    96→            )
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        age_days = (now - seen_dt).days
   100→        if age_days > max_age_days:
   101→            finding["status"] = "auto_resolved"
   102→            finding["resolved_at"] = now.isoformat()
   103→            finding["note"] = "holistic review expired — re-run review to re-evaluate"
   104→            expired.append(finding_id)
   105→
   106→    return expired
   107→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
     9→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    10→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.context import QueueContext
    13→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    14→    ALL_STATUSES,
    15→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    16→    scope_matches,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import (
    19→    build_communicate_score_item,
    20→    build_create_plan_item,
    21→    build_import_scores_item,
    22→    build_score_checkpoint_item,
    23→    build_subjective_items,
    24→    build_triage_stage_items,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    27→    build_finding_items,
    28→    enrich_with_impact,
    29→    group_queue_items,
    30→    item_explain,
    31→    item_sort_key,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    34→    _enrich_plan_metadata,
    35→    _filter_cluster_focus,
    36→    _separate_skipped,
    37→    _stamp_plan_sort_keys,
    38→    _stamp_positions,
    39→    collapse_clusters,
    40→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    41→)
    42→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    43→
    44→# Sentinel: "read scan_path from state" (the safe default).
    45→# Callers that want to override can pass an explicit str or None.
    46→_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE = object()
    47→
    48→
    49→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    50→class QueueBuildOptions:
    51→    """Configuration for queue construction.
    52→
    53→    ``scan_path`` defaults to reading from ``state["scan_path"]`` so callers
    54→    don't need to thread it manually.  Pass an explicit ``str`` or ``None``
    55→    to override (``None`` disables scope filtering).
    56→    """
    57→
    58→    count: int | None = 1
    59→    scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
    60→    scope: str | None = None
    61→    status: str = "open"
    62→    include_subjective: bool = True
    63→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    64→    chronic: bool = False
    65→    explain: bool = False
    66→    plan: dict | None = None
    67→    include_skipped: bool = False
    68→    cluster: str | None = None
    69→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
    70→    context: QueueContext | None = None
    71→
    72→
    73→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    74→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    75→
    76→    items: list[dict]
    77→    total: int
    78→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    79→    new_ids: set[str]
    80→
    81→
    82→def build_work_queue(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    86→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    87→    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.
    88→
    89→    Pipeline
    90→    --------
    91→    1. **Build finding items** — filter by status/scope/chronic, apply
    92→       per-detector standalone_threshold (evidence-only gating).
    93→    2. **Subjective items** — build synthetic dimension items, gate
    94→       non-initial ones behind SubjectiveVisibility policy (unless
    95→       the plan explicitly queued them).
    96→    3. **Workflow items** — inject triage stages, score checkpoint,
    97→       and create-plan items when a plan is active.
    98→    4. **Impact enrichment** — estimate per-item score impact from
    99→       dimension_scores; drop mechanical findings below impact floor.
   100→    5. **Plan ordering** — enrich with plan metadata, stamp sort keys
```

```
100→    5. **Plan ordering** — enrich with plan metadata, stamp sort keys
   101→       (``_plan_position`` / ``_is_new``), separate skipped items.
   102→    6. **Sort** — single ``item_sort_key`` pass: planned items by
   103→       position, then existing by natural rank, then new items.
   104→    7. **Post-sort** — re-append skipped if requested, stamp
   105→       ``queue_position``, filter to active cluster focus.
   106→    8. **Count limit** — truncate to ``options.count``.
   107→
   108→    Callers needing collapsed cluster display should call
   109→    ``collapse_clusters(items, plan)`` on the returned items.
   110→    """
   111→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   112→
   113→    # When a QueueContext is provided, its plan and policy take precedence
   114→    # over individually-set fields — the context is the single source of truth.
   115→    # Callers should not set both context and plan/policy on the same options.
   116→    ctx = resolved_options.context
   117→    effective_plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else resolved_options.plan
   118→
   119→    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
   120→    scan_path: str | None = (
   121→        state.get("scan_path")
   122→        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   123→        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    status = resolved_options.status
   127→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   129→    try:
   130→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   131→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   132→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   133→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   134→
   135→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   136→        state,
   137→        scan_path=scan_path,
   138→        status_filter=status,
   139→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   140→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   144→
   145→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
   146→    # QueueContext.policy is pre-computed with correct target_strict/plan;
   147→    # fall back to resolved_options.policy or on-the-fly computation.
   148→    policy = (
   149→        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
   150→        or resolved_options.policy
   151→        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=effective_plan)
   152→    )
   153→
   154→    if (
   155→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   156→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   157→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   158→    ):
   159→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   160→            state,
   161→            state.get("findings", {}),
   162→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   163→        )
   164→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   165→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   166→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   167→            set(effective_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   168→            if effective_plan
   169→            else set()
   170→        )
   171→        for item in subjective_items:
   172→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   173→                continue
   174→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   175→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   176→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   177→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   178→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   179→                continue
   180→            all_items.append(item)
   181→
   182→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   183→    if effective_plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   184→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(effective_plan, state)
   185→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   186→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(effective_plan, state)
   187→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   188→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   189→        import_scores_item = build_import_scores_item(effective_plan, state)
   190→        if import_scores_item is not None:
   191→            all_items.append(import_scores_item)
   192→        communicate_item = build_communicate_score_item(effective_plan, state)
   193→        if communicate_item is not None:
   194→            all_items.append(communicate_item)
   195→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(effective_plan)
   196→        if plan_item is not None:
   197→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   198→
   199→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   200→
   201→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   202→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   203→    all_items = [
   204→        item for item in all_items
   205→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   206→        or item.get("is_review")
   207→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   208→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   209→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   210→    ]
   211→
   212→    # Plan-aware pipeline: enrich, stamp sort keys, separate skipped,
   213→    # then a single sort produces the correct ordering.
   214→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   215→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   216→    if effective_plan:
   217→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   218→        _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, effective_plan)
   219→        _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, effective_plan, new_ids)
   220→        all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
   221→            all_items, effective_plan,
   222→        )
   223→
   224→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   225→
   226→    if effective_plan:
   227→        if resolved_options.include_skipped:
   228→            all_items = all_items + skipped_items
   229→        _stamp_positions(all_items, effective_plan)
   230→        all_items = _filter_cluster_focus(
   231→            all_items, effective_plan, resolved_options.cluster,
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    # Inject "run scan" workflow item when queue is empty and a plan cycle
   235→    # is active (plan_start_scores exist).  This gives the user an explicit
   236→    # next-step rather than a bare "nothing to do" message.
   237→    if not all_items and effective_plan:
   238→        plan_scores = effective_plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   239→        if plan_scores.get("strict") is not None:
   240→            all_items.append({
   241→                "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   242→                "kind": "workflow_action",
   243→                "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   244→                "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   245→                "file": "",
   246→                "detector": "workflow",
   247→                "confidence": "high",
   248→            })
   249→
   250→    total = len(all_items)
   251→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   252→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   253→
   254→    if resolved_options.explain:
   255→        for item in all_items:
   256→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   257→
   258→    return {
   259→        "items": all_items,
   260→        "total": total,
   261→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   262→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   263→    }
   264→
   265→
   266→__all__ = [
   267→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   268→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   269→    "QueueContext",
   270→    "WorkQueueResult",
   271→    "build_work_queue",
   272→    "collapse_clusters",
   273→    "group_queue_items",
   274→]
   275→
```

```
1→"""Predicates and small utilities for work queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    11→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    12→)
    13→
    14→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    15→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    16→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    17→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    18→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    19→)
    20→
    21→
    22→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    23→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    24→    detail = item.get("detail")
    25→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    26→
    27→
    28→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    29→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    30→
    31→
    32→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    33→    detector = item.get("detector")
    34→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    35→        return True
    36→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    37→        return True
    38→    return False
    39→
    40→
    41→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    42→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    47→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    48→        return True
    49→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    50→        members = item.get("members", [])
    51→        return bool(members) and all(
    52→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    53→        )
    54→    return False
    55→
    56→
    57→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    58→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    59→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    60→    weight_by_confidence = {
    61→        "high": 1.0,
    62→        "medium": 0.7,
    63→        "low": 0.3,
    64→    }
    65→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    66→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    67→        weight *= 10.0
    68→    return float(weight)
    69→
    70→
    71→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    72→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    73→    if not scope:
    74→        return True
    75→
    76→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    77→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    78→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    79→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    80→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    81→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    82→
    83→    if "*" in scope:
    84→        return any(
    85→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    86→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    87→        )
    88→
    89→    if "::" in scope:
    90→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    91→
    92→    lowered = scope.lower()
    93→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    94→        return (
    95→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    96→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    97→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    98→        )
    99→
   100→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
```

```
1→"""Unified queue-resolution context.
     2→
     3→A frozen ``QueueContext`` computed once per operation replaces the scattered
     4→``plan`` / ``target_strict`` / ``policy`` threading through function chains.
     5→Callers build one context and pass it everywhere — makes the wrong thing
     6→impossible.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from dataclasses import dataclass
    12→
    13→from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    14→    SubjectiveVisibility,
    15→    compute_subjective_visibility,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→# Sentinel: "auto-load plan from disk" (the default).
    20→_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD = object()
    21→
    22→
    23→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    24→class QueueContext:
    25→    """Immutable snapshot of resolved queue parameters.
    26→
    27→    Built once via :func:`queue_context`, then threaded through
    28→    ``build_work_queue``, ``plan_aware_queue_breakdown``, and command
    29→    helpers so every call site agrees on plan, target, and policy.
    30→    """
    31→
    32→    plan: dict | None
    33→    target_strict: float
    34→    policy: SubjectiveVisibility
    35→
    36→
    37→def queue_context(
    38→    state: StateModel,
    39→    *,
    40→    config: dict | None = None,
    41→    plan: dict | None | object = _PLAN_AUTO_LOAD,
    42→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    43→) -> QueueContext:
    44→    """Build a :class:`QueueContext` with all parameters resolved.
    45→
    46→    Resolution order:
    47→
    48→    1. **plan** — explicit value wins; sentinel ``_PLAN_AUTO_LOAD`` triggers
    49→       ``load_plan()`` (guarded by ``PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS``).
    50→    2. **target_strict** — explicit float wins; ``None`` reads from *config*
```

```
    17→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    18→class ResolvedEntity:
    19→    """Classifies what the user's show pattern refers to."""
    20→
    21→    kind: str  # "dimension", "special_view", "file_or_pattern"
    22→    pattern: str  # Original user pattern
    23→    display_name: str  # Human-readable label
    24→    detectors: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # Detector names (for dimension kind)
    25→    is_subjective: bool = False  # Whether this is a subjective dimension
    26→
    27→
    28→def _resolve_special_view(pattern: str, lowered: str) -> ResolvedEntity | None:
    29→    if lowered not in ("concerns", "subjective", "subjective_review"):
    30→        return None
    31→    return ResolvedEntity(
    32→        kind="special_view",
```

```
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))

    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True

    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:

    40→    # When policy.scan_path is explicitly set, override the auto-default.

    42→    scan_path_kwarg: dict = (

    44→    )
    45→    return build_work_queue(
    46→        state,
    47→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    48→            count=policy.count,
    49→            status="open",
    50→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
```

```python
             file: str = "test.py", detail: dict | None = None) -> dict:

        "detector": detector,
        "file": file,
        "tier": tier,
        "confidence": "high",
        "summary": f"Finding {fid}",
        "status": "open",
        "detail": detail or {},

def _state_with(*findings: dict) -> dict:

    return {"findings": fmap, "scan_count": 5}
```

## 5. WORK QUEUE PIPELINE ARCHITECTURE

### **Complete Flow: `build_work_queue(state, options=None)`**

The pipeline in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` (lines 82-263):

**Stage 1: Input Resolution**
- Resolve `QueueBuildOptions` (defaults applied)
- Read `scan_path` from state if sentinel `_SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE` passed
- Validate status filter against `ALL_STATUSES`
- Clamp subjective_threshold to [0, 100]

**Stage 2: Build Finding Items** (line 135)
- Calls `build_finding_items()` with:
  - Status filter (open/fixed/all/custom)
  - Scope pattern matching
  - Chronic mode reopen_count filtering
  - Detector-level `standalone_threshold` gating (evidence-only)

**Stage 3: Add Subjective Dimension Items** (lines 154-180)
- Build synthetic items via `build_subjective_items()`
- Apply `SubjectiveVisibility` policy for gating:
  - Stale items blocked while objective backlog exists
  - Unassessed (placeholder) items always surface
  - Non-initial items gated by policy
- **Override:** Items in `plan["queue_order"]` surface regardless of policy

**Stage 4: Inject Workflow Items** (lines 182-197)
- When plan is active, add:
  - `build_triage_stage_items()` - epic triage items
  - `build_score_checkpoint_item()` - checkpoint marker
  - `build_import_scores_item()` - import scores action
  - `build_communicate_score_item()` - communicate action
  - `build_create_plan_item()` - create plan action

**Stage 5: Impact Enrichment** (line 199)
- `enrich_with_impact()` computes `estimated_impact` from dimension_scores
- Dimension headroom = (100 - current_strict_score) × issues / potential
- Higher headroom = higher impact

**Stage 6: Impact Floor Filtering** (lines 201-210)
- Drop mechanical findings with `estimated_impact < 0.05`
- Keep all non-findings (subjective, synthetic, workflow items)
- Fallback: items without dimension_scores get impact 0.0 and survive

**Stage 7: Plan-Aware Enrichment** (lines 212-222)
- `_enrich_plan_metadata()` - attach plan cluster/position info
- `_stamp_plan_sort_keys()` - compute `_plan_position` / `_is_new`
- `_separate_skipped()` - extract skipped items

**Stage 8: Single Sort Pass** (line 224)
- `item_sort_key()` tiebreaker order:
  1. Planned items by `_plan_position` (ascending)
  2. Existing items by natural rank
  3. New items by natural rank

**Stage 9: Post-Sort** (lines 226-232)
- Re-append skipped items if `include_skipped=True`
- `_stamp_positions()` - add `queue_position` field
- `_filter_cluster_focus()` - if `cluster=X`, show only cluster X members

**Stage 10: Special Case — Empty Queue with Active Plan** (lines 234-248)
- If queue is empty but plan has `plan_start_scores`:
  - Inject `workflow::run-scan` item to prompt user

**Stage 11: Count Limiting & Explain** (lines 250-256)
- Truncate to `options.count` if specified
- Add `explain` dict with `ranking_factors` if requested

**Return Value: `WorkQueueResult`**
```python
{
    "items": list[dict],  # Ranked items (findings, subjective, synthetic)
    "total": int,         # Total before count limit
    "grouped": dict,      # Items grouped by file/kind
    "new_ids": set[str]   # IDs not in prior scan
}
```

---

## 6. KEY ABSTRACTIONS & TYPES

### **QueueBuildOptions** (dataclass)
```python
count: int | None = 1
scan_path: str | None | object = _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE  # Sentinel
scope: str | None = None
status: str = "open"
include_subjective: bool = True
subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
chronic: bool = False
explain: bool = False
plan: dict | None = None
include_skipped: bool = False
cluster: str | None = None
policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
context: QueueContext | None = None
```

### **QueueContext** (frozen dataclass)
```python
plan: dict | None
target_strict: float
policy: SubjectiveVisibility
```
Built via `queue_context(state, config, plan, target_strict)` for unified resolution.

### **WorkQueueResult** (TypedDict)
```python
items: list[dict]
total: int
grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
new_ids: set[str]
```

### **Item Kinds in Queue**
- `"finding"` - detector findings (mechanical)
- `"subjective_dimension"` - synthetic dimension items
- `"cluster"` - collapsed multiple findings
- `"workflow_action"` - triage/checkpoint/import items
- `"review_finding"` - review detector items

---

## 7. CALLER PATTERNS & RESPONSIBILITIES

### **Quick-reference caller responsibilities:**

| Caller | Responsibility | Key Imports |
|--------|---|---|
| `next.py` | Show next item(s) to work on | `build_work_queue`, `queue_context` |
| `queue_guard.py` | Enforce plan queue order in resolve | `build_work_queue`, `collapse_clusters` |
| `plan/queue_render.py` | Render plan queue view | `build_work_queue`, `collapse_clusters` |
| `show/scope.py` | Filter queue by pattern | `build_work_queue` |
| `scan_workflow.py` | Collect review findings post-scan | `list_open_review_findings` |
| `queue_progress.py` | Compute queue statistics | `build_work_queue`, `is_subjective_queue_item` |
| `planning/render.py` | Scorecard/ranking rendering | `build_work_queue` |
| `planning/select.py` | Legacy `get_next_item()` API | `build_work_queue` |
| Tests | Validate queue construction & filtering | `build_work_queue`, fixtures |

---

## SUMMARY

The work queue pipeline is a **9-stage** transformation that:
1. Ingests findings + dimension scores from state
2. Filters by status/scope/chronic/evidence
3. Adds synthetic subjective items with gating logic
4. Injects workflow items from active plan
5. Ranks by estimated dimension impact
6. Applies impact floor (0.05 minimum)
7. Respects plan ordering when present
8. Performs single deterministic sort
9. Returns count-limited, grouped items

**25 modules** call the public work_queue API across commands, planning, and test suites. **Test coverage** includes 400+ test cases in `work_queue_cases.py` plus integration variants, guard tests, and context tests. The facade (`work_queue.py`) exports **23 public symbols** re-exported from 6 internal submodules.

> DEVELOPER

I'm refactoring the work queue pipeline in `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/` to make it beautiful. I've already done a structural cleanup (phases 1-6) and now I want to make the code genuinely elegant.

Here's the current state of the key files and the specific ugliness I want to fix:

## Current `core.py:build_work_queue` (the orchestrator)
It's ~160 lines with everything inline. The pipeline *shape* is obscured by detail. Here's the structure:
- 10 lines: option resolution (QueueBuildOptions defaults, context resolution)
- 10 lines: scan_path/status/threshold resolution + validation
- 5 lines: call build_finding_items
- 10 lines: policy resolution (ctx.policy or opts.policy or compute)
- 25 lines: subjective item gating (build items, filter by policy, plan override)
- 15 lines: workflow item injection (5 builders, each with if-not-None guard)
- 3 lines: impact enrichment
- 8 lines: impact floor list comprehension
- 10 lines: plan enrichment + stamp sort keys + separate skipped
- 1 line: sort
- 8 lines: post-sort (re-append skipped, stamp positions, filter cluster)
- 14 lines: empty queue "run scan" injection
- 8 lines: count limit + explain
- 5 lines: return dict

## Current `ranking.py:item_sort_key` and `_natural_sort_key`
Uses magic numbers for sort tiers: `-3`, `-2`, `-1`, `0`, `1`, `2`. You need a mental lookup table to understand the ordering contract.

## Current `plan_order.py` exports
Exports `_enrich_plan_metadata`, `_stamp_plan_sort_keys`, `_separate_skipped`, `_filter_cluster_focus`, `_stamp_positions` — private-named functions used cross-module from `core.py`. The underscore prefix is misleading.

## My proposed changes:

### 1. Extract pipeline steps from `build_work_queue` into named private functions in `core.py`
The orchestrator should read like a table of contents:
```python
def build_work_queue(state, *, options=None):
    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.

    Pipeline:
    1. Gather — finding items, subjective dimensions, workflow stages
    2. Score  — estimate impact from dimension headroom, apply floor
    3. Order  — stamp plan positions, sort, filter to cluster focus
    4. Limit  — truncate to count, optionally add explain metadata
    """
    opts = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    plan, policy = _resolve_plan_and_policy(opts, state)

    # 1. Gather
    items = _gather_finding_items(state, opts)
    items += _gather_subjective_items(state, opts, plan, policy)
    items += _gather_workflow_items(state, plan, opts.status)

    # 2. Score & filter
    enrich_with_impact(items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
    items = [i for i in items if _passes_impact_floor(i)]

    # 3. Plan-aware ordering
    new_ids, skipped = _apply_plan_pipeline(items, state, plan)
    items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
    _finalize_plan_order(items, skipped, plan, opts)

    # 4. Finalize
    items += _maybe_run_scan_item(items, plan)
    total = len(items)
    if opts.count is not None and opts.count > 0:
        items = items[:opts.count]
    if opts.explain:
        for item in items:
            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)

    return {"items": items, "total": total, "grouped": group_queue_items(items, "item"), "new_ids": new_ids}
```

Each extracted function is 10-25 lines, lives in the same file, does one thing.

### 2. Named sort tier constants in `ranking.py`
```python
# Plan-aware sort tiers
_PLANNED = 0     # Items with explicit plan position
_EXISTING = 1    # Known items, natural ranking  
_NEW = 2         # Newly discovered items

# Natural ranking groups (lower = higher priority)
_INITIAL_REVIEW = -3   # Unassessed subjective dimensions
_TRIAGE_STAGE = -2     # Epic triage workflow stages
_WORKFLOW_ACTION = -1  # Score checkpoints, create-plan
_CLUSTER = 0           # Auto-clustered findings
_FINDING = 1           # Individual findings + assessed subjective
```

Then `_natural_sort_key` and `item_sort_key` use these instead of bare numbers.

### 3. Drop underscore prefixes in `plan_order.py`
Rename: `_enrich_plan_metadata` → `enrich_plan_metadata`, etc. for all 5 functions.
Update imports in `core.py`, `apply_plan_order` wrapper, and `__all__`.

### 4. Group QueueBuildOptions fields with comments

```
    82→def build_work_queue(
    83→    state: StateModel,
    84→    *,
    85→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    86→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    87→    """Build a ranked work queue from state findings.
    88→
    89→    Pipeline
    90→    --------
    91→    1. **Build finding items** — filter by status/scope/chronic, apply
    92→       per-detector standalone_threshold (evidence-only gating).
    93→    2. **Subjective items** — build synthetic dimension items, gate
    94→       non-initial ones behind SubjectiveVisibility policy (unless
    95→       the plan explicitly queued them).
    96→    3. **Workflow items** — inject triage stages, score checkpoint,
    97→       and create-plan items when a plan is active.
    98→    4. **Impact enrichment** — estimate per-item score impact from
    99→       dimension_scores; drop mechanical findings below impact floor.
   100→    5. **Plan ordering** — enrich with plan metadata, stamp sort keys
   101→       (``_plan_position`` / ``_is_new``), separate skipped items.
   102→    6. **Sort** — single ``item_sort_key`` pass: planned items by
   103→       position, then existing by natural rank, then new items.
   104→    7. **Post-sort** — re-append skipped if requested, stamp
   105→       ``queue_position``, filter to active cluster focus.
   106→    8. **Count limit** — truncate to ``options.count``.
   107→
   108→    Callers needing collapsed cluster display should call
   109→    ``collapse_clusters(items, plan)`` on the returned items.
   110→    """
   111→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   112→
   113→    # When a QueueContext is provided, its plan and policy take precedence
   114→    # over individually-set fields — the context is the single source of truth.
   115→    # Callers should not set both context and plan/policy on the same options.
   116→    ctx = resolved_options.context
   117→    effective_plan = ctx.plan if ctx is not None else resolved_options.plan
   118→
   119→    # Resolve scan_path: sentinel → read from state, explicit value → use as-is
   120→    scan_path: str | None = (
   121→        state.get("scan_path")
   122→        if resolved_options.scan_path is _SCAN_PATH_FROM_STATE
   123→        else resolved_options.scan_path  # type: ignore[assignment]
   124→    )
   125→
   126→    status = resolved_options.status
   127→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   128→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   129→    try:
   130→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   131→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   132→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   133→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   134→
   135→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   136→        state,
   137→        scan_path=scan_path,
   138→        status_filter=status,
   139→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   140→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   141→    )
   142→
   143→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   144→
   145→    # Resolve subjective visibility policy for gating.
   146→    # QueueContext.policy is pre-computed with correct target_strict/plan;
   147→    # fall back to resolved_options.policy or on-the-fly computation.
   148→    policy = (
   149→        (ctx.policy if ctx is not None else None)
   150→        or resolved_options.policy
   151→        or compute_subjective_visibility(state, plan=effective_plan)
   152→    )
   153→
   154→    if (
   155→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   156→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   157→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   158→    ):
   159→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   160→            state,
   161→            state.get("findings", {}),
   162→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   163→        )
   164→        # When a plan explicitly includes a subjective item in queue_order,
   165→        # surface it regardless of policy — the plan is authoritative.
   166→        plan_queue_set: set[str] = (
   167→            set(effective_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   168→            if effective_plan
   169→            else set()
   170→        )
   171→        for item in subjective_items:
   172→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   173→                continue
   174→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   175→            # backlog is drained — unless the plan explicitly queued them
   176→            # (e.g. stale dims injected after a completed cycle).
   177→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   178→            if not policy.should_surface(item) and item_id not in plan_queue_set:
   179→                continue
   180→            all_items.append(item)
   181→
   182→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   183→    if effective_plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   184→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(effective_plan, state)
   185→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   186→        checkpoint_item = build_score_checkpoint_item(effective_plan, state)
   187→        if checkpoint_item is not None:
   188→            all_items.append(checkpoint_item)
   189→        import_scores_item = build_import_scores_item(effective_plan, state)
   190→        if import_scores_item is not None:
   191→            all_items.append(import_scores_item)
   192→        communicate_item = build_communicate_score_item(effective_plan, state)
   193→        if communicate_item is not None:
   194→            all_items.append(communicate_item)
   195→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(effective_plan)
   196→        if plan_item is not None:
   197→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   198→
   199→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   200→
   201→    # Impact floor: drop mechanical findings with negligible score impact
   202→    MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT = 0.05
   203→    all_items = [
   204→        item for item in all_items
   205→        if item.get("kind") != "finding"
   206→        or item.get("is_review")
   207→        or item.get("is_subjective")
   208→        or not item.get("estimated_impact")
   209→        or item["estimated_impact"] >= MIN_STANDALONE_IMPACT
   210→    ]
   211→
   212→    # Plan-aware pipeline: enrich, stamp sort keys, separate skipped,
   213→    # then a single sort produces the correct ordering.
   214→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   215→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   216→    if effective_plan:
   217→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   218→        _enrich_plan_metadata(all_items, effective_plan)
   219→        _stamp_plan_sort_keys(all_items, effective_plan, new_ids)
   220→        all_items, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(
   221→            all_items, effective_plan,
   222→        )
   223→
   224→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   225→
   226→    if effective_plan:
   227→        if resolved_options.include_skipped:
   228→            all_items = all_items + skipped_items
   229→        _stamp_positions(all_items, effective_plan)
   230→        all_items = _filter_cluster_focus(
   231→            all_items, effective_plan, resolved_options.cluster,
   232→        )
   233→
   234→    # Inject "run scan" workflow item when queue is empty and a plan cycle
   235→    # is active (plan_start_scores exist).  This gives the user an explicit
   236→    # next-step rather than a bare "nothing to do" message.
   237→    if not all_items and effective_plan:
   238→        plan_scores = effective_plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   239→        if plan_scores.get("strict") is not None:
   240→            all_items.append({
   241→                "id": "workflow::run-scan",
   242→                "kind": "workflow_action",
   243→                "summary": "Queue cleared \u2014 run scan to finalize and reveal your updated score.",
   244→                "primary_command": "desloppify scan",
   245→                "file": "",
   246→                "detector": "workflow",
   247→                "confidence": "high",
   248→            })
   249→
   250→    total = len(all_items)
   251→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   252→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   253→
   254→    if resolved_options.explain:
   255→        for item in all_items:
   256→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   257→
   258→    return {
   259→        "items": all_items,
   260→        "total": total,
   261→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   262→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   263→    }
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.synthetic import subjective_strict_scores
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    81→
    82→    return 0.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    86→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    88→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    89→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    90→
    91→
    92→def build_finding_items(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    *,
    95→    scan_path: str | None,
    96→    status_filter: str,
    97→    scope: str | None,
    98→    chronic: bool,
    99→) -> list[dict]:
   100→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   101→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   102→    out: list[dict] = []
   103→
   104→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   105→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   106→            continue
   107→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   108→            continue
   109→        if chronic and not (
   110→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   111→        ):
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   115→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   116→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   117→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   118→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   119→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   120→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   121→                continue
   122→
   123→        item = dict(finding)
   124→        item["id"] = finding_id
   125→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   126→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   127→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   128→        item["review_weight"] = (
   129→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   130→        )
   131→        subjective_score = None
   132→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   133→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   134→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   135→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   136→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   137→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   138→            )
   139→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   140→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   141→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   142→            item,
   143→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   144→        )
   145→
   146→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   147→            continue
   148→        out.append(item)
   149→
   150→    return out
   151→
   152→
   153→def _natural_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   154→    """Compute natural (non-plan-aware) ranking for queue items."""
   155→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   156→
   157→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   158→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   159→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   162→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   163→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   164→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   165→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   166→
   167→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   168→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   169→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   170→
   171→    if kind == "cluster":
   172→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   173→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   174→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   175→        )
   176→        return (
   177→            0,
   178→            action_pri,
   179→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   180→            item.get("id", ""),
   181→        )
   182→
   183→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   184→
   185→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   186→        return (
   187→            1,
   188→            -impact,
   189→            subjective_score_value(item),
   190→            item.get("id", ""),
   191→        )
   192→
   193→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   194→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   195→    return (
   196→        1,
   197→        -impact,
   198→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   199→        -review_weight,
   200→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   201→        item.get("id", ""),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→
   205→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   206→    """Unified sort key: plan position first, then natural ranking.
   207→
   208→    When ``_plan_position`` is stamped (by :func:`_stamp_plan_sort_keys`),
   209→    planned items sort first in plan order, then existing items by natural
   210→    ranking, then newly-discovered items by natural ranking.
   211→
   212→    When no plan fields are stamped, falls back to pure natural ranking.
   213→    """
   214→    plan_pos = item.get("_plan_position")
   215→
   216→    if plan_pos is not None:
   217→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   218→        # Triage stages: even when explicitly planned, maintain the
   219→        # blocked-before-unblocked invariant for correctness.
   220→        if kind == "workflow_stage":
   221→            blocked = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   222→            stage_idx = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   223→            return (0, blocked, stage_idx, item.get("id", ""))
   224→        return (0, 0, plan_pos, item.get("id", ""))
   225→
   226→    is_new = item.get("_is_new", False)
   227→    group = 2 if is_new else 1
   228→    return (group, *_natural_sort_key(item))
   229→
   230→
   231→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   232→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   233→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   234→        return {
   235→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   236→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   237→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   238→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   239→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   240→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   241→        }
   242→
   243→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   244→        return {
   245→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   246→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after triage stages, before findings.",
   247→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   248→        }
   249→
   250→    if kind == "cluster":
   251→        return {
   252→            "kind": "cluster",
   253→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   254→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   255→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   256→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   257→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   258→        }
   259→
   260→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   261→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   262→        return {
   263→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   264→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   265→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   266→            "initial_review": initial,
   267→            "policy": (
   268→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   269→                if initial else
   270→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   271→            ),
   272→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   273→        }
   274→
   275→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   276→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   277→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   278→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   279→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   280→    if is_subjective:
   281→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   282→    elif is_review:
   283→        ranking_factors = [
   284→            "estimated_impact desc",
   285→            "confidence asc",
   286→            "review_weight desc",
   287→            "count desc",
   288→            "id asc",
   289→        ]
   290→    else:
   291→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   292→    explain = {
   293→        "kind": "finding",
   294→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   295→        "confidence": confidence,
   296→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   297→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   298→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   299→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   300→    }
   301→    if is_review:
   302→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   303→    if is_subjective:
   304→        explain["policy"] = (
   305→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   306→        )
   307→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   308→    return explain
   309→
   310→
   311→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   312→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   313→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   314→    for item in items:
   315→        if group == "file":
   316→            key = item.get("file", "")
   317→        elif group == "detector":
   318→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   319→        elif group == "cluster":
   320→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   321→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   322→        else:
   323→            key = "items"
   324→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   325→    return grouped
   326→
   327→
   328→__all__ = [
   329→    "build_finding_items",
   330→    "enrich_with_impact",
   331→    "item_explain",
   332→    "item_sort_key",
   333→    "subjective_score_value",
   334→    "group_queue_items",
   335→]
   336→
```

```
1→"""Plan-order and cluster-collapse helpers for work queues."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.state import StateModel
     6→
     7→
     8→def new_item_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
     9→    """Return finding IDs added in the most recent scan."""
    10→    scan_history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    11→    if not scan_history:
    12→        return set()
    13→    threshold = scan_history[-1].get("timestamp", "")
    14→    if not threshold:
    15→        return set()
    16→    return {
    17→        finding_id
    18→        for finding_id, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    19→        if finding.get("first_seen", "") >= threshold
    20→    }
    21→
    22→
    23→def _enrich_plan_metadata(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp plan description, note, and cluster info from overrides."""
    25→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    26→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    27→
    28→    for item in items:
    29→        override = overrides.get(item["id"], {})
    30→        if override.get("description"):
    31→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    32→        if override.get("note"):
    33→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    34→        if override.get("cluster"):
    35→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    36→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    37→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    38→                "name": cluster_name,
    39→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    40→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    41→            }
    42→
    43→
    44→def _stamp_plan_sort_keys(
    45→    items: list[dict],
    46→    plan: dict,
    47→    new_ids: set[str],
    48→) -> None:
    49→    """Stamp ``_plan_position`` and ``_is_new`` on each item.
    50→
    51→    These fields are consumed by :func:`item_sort_key` in ``ranking.py``
    52→    to produce the correct final ordering in a single sort pass.
    53→    """
    54→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    55→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    56→
    57→    position_map: dict[str, int] = {}
    58→    for idx, finding_id in enumerate(queue_order):
    59→        if finding_id not in skipped_ids:
    60→            position_map[finding_id] = idx
    61→
    62→    for item in items:
    63→        item_id = item["id"]
    64→        pos = position_map.get(item_id)
    65→        item["_plan_position"] = pos  # None if not in queue_order
    66→        item["_is_new"] = item_id in new_ids
    67→
    68→
    69→def _separate_skipped(
    70→    items: list[dict],
    71→    plan: dict,
    72→) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
    73→    """Separate skipped items from the main list.
    74→
    75→    Returns ``(non_skipped, skipped)`` so callers can optionally re-append.
    76→    """
    77→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    78→    if not skipped_ids:
    79→        return items, []
    80→    non_skipped: list[dict] = []
    81→    skipped: list[dict] = []
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
    84→            skipped.append(item)
    85→        else:
    86→            non_skipped.append(item)
    87→    return non_skipped, skipped
    88→
    89→
    90→def _filter_cluster_focus(
    91→    items: list[dict],
    92→    plan: dict,
    93→    cluster: str | None,
    94→) -> list[dict]:
    95→    """Filter to only cluster members when a cluster focus is active."""
    96→    effective_cluster = cluster or plan.get("active_cluster")
    97→    if not effective_cluster:
    98→        return items
    99→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   100→    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   101→    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   102→    if not cluster_member_ids:
   103→        return items
   104→    return [item for item in items if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   105→
   106→
   107→def _stamp_positions(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> None:
   108→    """Stamp queue_position and plan_skipped metadata on each item."""
   109→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   110→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   111→
   112→    for position, item in enumerate(items):
   113→        item["queue_position"] = position + 1
   114→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   115→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   116→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   117→            if skip_entry:
   118→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   119→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   120→                if skip_reason:
   121→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   122→
   123→
   124→def apply_plan_order(
   125→    items: list[dict],
   126→    plan: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   129→    cluster: str | None = None,
   130→    new_ids: set[str] | None = None,
   131→) -> list[dict]:
   132→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   133→
   134→    Thin wrapper that calls the decomposed pipeline steps in order.
   135→    Uses sort-key stamping so a single :func:`item_sort_key` pass
   136→    produces the correct ordering.
   137→    """
   138→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import item_sort_key
   139→
   140→    _enrich_plan_metadata(items, plan)
   141→    _stamp_plan_sort_keys(items, plan, new_ids or set())
   142→    result, skipped_items = _separate_skipped(items, plan)
   143→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   144→    if include_skipped:
   145→        result = result + skipped_items
   146→    _stamp_positions(result, plan)
   147→    result = _filter_cluster_focus(result, plan, cluster)
   148→    return result
   149→
   150→
   151→def action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   152→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   153→    try:
   154→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   155→
   156→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   157→        if meta:
   158→            return meta.action_type
   159→    except ImportError as exc:
   160→        _ = exc
   161→    return "manual_fix"
   162→
   163→
   164→def _build_cluster_meta(
   165→    cluster_name: str, members: list[dict], cluster_data: dict
   166→) -> dict:
   167→    """Build a cluster meta-item from its member items."""
   168→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   169→    action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   170→    if "desloppify autofix" in action:
   171→        action_type = "auto_fix"
   172→    elif "desloppify move" in action:
   173→        action_type = "reorganize"
   174→    else:
   175→        action_type = action_type_for_detector(detector)
   176→        if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify autofix" not in action:
   177→            action_type = "refactor"
   178→
   179→    stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   180→    total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   181→    if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   182→        summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   183→    else:
   184→        summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   185→
   186→    primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   187→    if not primary_command:
   188→        primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10"
   189→
   190→    estimated_impact = max(
   191→        (m.get("estimated_impact", 0.0) for m in members), default=0.0
   192→    )
   193→
   194→    return {
   195→        "id": cluster_name,
   196→        "kind": "cluster",
   197→        "action_type": action_type,
   198→        "summary": summary,
   199→        "members": members,
   200→        "member_count": len(members),
   201→        "primary_command": primary_command,
   202→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
   203→        "cluster_auto": True,
   204→        "cluster_optional": bool(cluster_data.get("optional")),
   205→        "confidence": "high",
   206→        "detector": detector,
   207→        "file": "",
   208→        "estimated_impact": estimated_impact,
   209→    }
   210→
   211→
   212→def collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   213→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   214→
   215→    Walks the list in order: the first member of each collapsed cluster is
   216→    replaced with its meta-item, subsequent members are skipped.  This
   217→    preserves the ordering established by sort + plan-order.
   218→    """
   219→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   220→    if not clusters:
   221→        return items
   222→
   223→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   224→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   225→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   226→            continue
   227→        for finding_id in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   228→            fid_to_cluster[finding_id] = name
   229→
   230→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   231→        return items
   232→
   233→    # Collect members per cluster (preserving encounter order)
   234→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   235→    for item in items:
   236→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   237→        if cname:
   238→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   239→
   240→    # Build meta-items only for clusters with 2+ members in the queue
   241→    meta_items: dict[str, dict] = {}
   242→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   243→        if len(members) < 2:
   244→            continue
   245→        meta_items[cname] = _build_cluster_meta(
   246→            cname, members, clusters.get(cname, {})
   247→        )
   248→
   249→    # Walk in order: replace first member of each collapsed cluster
   250→    # with its meta-item, skip subsequent members
   251→    seen_clusters: set[str] = set()
   252→    result: list[dict] = []
   253→    for item in items:
   254→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   255→        if cname and cname in meta_items:
   256→            if cname not in seen_clusters:
   257→                seen_clusters.add(cname)
   258→                result.append(meta_items[cname])
   259→            # skip individual member
   260→        else:
   261→            result.append(item)
   262→    return result
   263→
   264→
   265→__all__ = [
   266→    "_enrich_plan_metadata",
   267→    "_filter_cluster_focus",
   268→    "_separate_skipped",
   269→    "_stamp_plan_sort_keys",
   270→    "_stamp_positions",
   271→    "apply_plan_order",
   272→    "collapse_clusters",
   273→    "new_item_ids",
   274→]
   275→
```
